### **DRAFT: FACILITATOR INDEPENDENT CONTRACTOR AGREEMENT**

**THIS AGREEMENT** is made on **[Date]**

**BETWEEN:**

**[Your Company Name Ltd.]**, a company registered in Ghana with its registered office at **[Your Company Address]** (hereinafter referred to as "**the Company**").

**AND**

**[Facilitator's Full Name]**, of **[Facilitator's Address]** (hereinafter referred to as "**the Contractor**").

**BACKGROUND:**

The Company is engaged in the business of providing robotics and coding education. The Company wishes to engage the services of the Contractor as a facilitator for its educational programmes, and the Contractor agrees to provide such services, on the terms and conditions set forth in this Agreement.

**IT IS HEREBY AGREED AS FOLLOWS:**

**1. INTERPRETATION**
The terms "Commission," "Equipment," "Operations Manual," "Oversight Manager," "Session," and "Zobase System" shall have the meanings ascribed to them in this Agreement and its Annexures.

**2. STATUS AS INDEPENDENT CONTRACTOR**
2.1. The Contractor is engaged as an independent contractor. This Agreement shall not be construed as creating a relationship of employer-employee, partnership, or joint venture between the Parties.
2.2. The Contractor acknowledges that they are solely responsible for their own income tax, National Health Insurance, and other statutory obligations, and are not entitled to any benefits provided by the Company to its employees, including but not limited to contributions under the Social Security and National Insurance Trust (SSNIT).
2.3. The Contractor has no authority to bind the Company in any way without prior written consent.

**3. ROLES, RESPONSIBILITIES & COMMISSION**
3.1. **Duties:** The Contractor shall:
    3.1.1. Facilitate robotics and coding Sessions for students in accordance with the curriculum, schedules, and procedures detailed in the Company's Operations Manual (Annexure A).
    3.1.2. Adhere strictly to the equipment management, check-in, and check-out procedures ("The John Protocol") as directed by the Oversight Manager.
    3.1.3. Represent the Company professionally at all times.
3.2. **Commission Structure:**
    3.2.1. The Contractor's remuneration shall be solely on a commission-only basis.
    3.2.2. The commission rate shall be **[e.g., 40%]** of the net fee received by the Company for each Session that is successfully completed by the Contractor.
    3.2.3. A Session is deemed "successfully completed" only upon the Oversight Manager's confirmation that: (i) the Session was delivered; (ii) all equipment was returned undamaged; and (iii) the post-session checklist has been signed.
3.3. **Oversight:** The Contractor shall report to and comply with all reasonable instructions from the Company's designated Oversight Manager ("John") regarding operational procedures.

**4. EQUIPMENT ACCOUNTABILITY & INTELLECTUAL PROPERTY**
4.1. The Contractor is financially accountable for any loss or damage to Company Equipment assigned to them. The Contractor agrees to reimburse the Company the full replacement value for any lost or damaged equipment, which may be deducted from accrued commissions.
4.2. All curriculum, training materials, processes, and any other intellectual property created by the Contractor in the course of, or related to, this Agreement shall be the sole and exclusive property of the Company. The Contractor hereby assigns all rights, title, and interest in such intellectual property to the Company.


5. Offsite Mismanagement & Misuse

5.1. The Contractor shall not use the Company's Equipment for any purpose other than the performance of the Services at the locations expressly prescribed by the Company in writing ("Prescribed Sites"). Any use of Equipment for personal projects, non-approved sessions, or at any location other than a Prescribed Site is strictly prohibited and shall constitute material misuse.

5.2. The Contractor assumes full financial responsibility for any loss, damage, or repair costs arising from misuse, improper handling, or use of the Equipment at a non-Prescribed Site. The Company is authorized to deduct the full replacement or repair value from the Contractor's pending commissions.

5.3. Any breach of this clause shall be deemed a material breach of this Agreement, entitling the Company to terminate the Agreement immediately and pursue any and all available legal remedies



6. Training Obligation (Main Facilitator)

6.1. The Contractor, in their capacity as Main Facilitator, agrees to provide practical training to all new facilitators engaged by the Company. This training shall ensure new personnel are fully equipped to perform their duties competently and in compliance with the Company's curriculum and operational protocols.

6.2. The training shall be tailored to the specific roles and responsibilities of a facilitator and shall include, but not be limited to, instruction on:
(a) The Company's prescribed curriculum and teaching methodologies;
(b) Equipment management, including the "John Protocol" for check-in and check-out procedures;
(c) All operational and safety procedures as outlined in the Company's Operations Manual.

6.3. The Main Facilitator shall perform these training duties at the Company's reasonable request and as an integral part of the Services rendered under this Agreement, for which the Main Facilitator's standard commission shall be the sole compensation.




**7. SCHEDULE OF FINES**
The Company may impose the following fines for operational breaches, which shall be deducted from the Contractor's commission:

| Breach | Fine Amount |
| :--- | :--- |
| Failure to clock in/out on the Zobase System for a Session without prior authorization. | [e.g., Ã¢â€šÂµ50] |
| Failure to complete the pre- or post-session equipment checklist. | [e.g., Ã¢â€šÂµ75] |
| Unauthorized use of Company Equipment or deviation from the prescribed curriculum. | [e.g., Ã¢â€šÂµ100] |
| Unauthorized use of a Session venue. | [e.g., Ã¢â€šÂµ200] |

**8. CONFIDENTIALITY & NON-SOLICITATION**
8.1. The Contractor shall not, during or after the termination of this Agreement, disclose any confidential information of the Company, including but not limited to business strategies, client lists, pricing, and curriculum.
8.2. **Non-Solicitation:** The Contractor shall not, for a period of **[e.g., 12 (twelve)]** months following the termination of this Agreement, directly or indirectly solicit or entice away any client, student, or employee of the Company.

**9. EXCLUSIVITY**
During the term of this Agreement, the Contractor shall not provide services, whether as an employee or contractor, to any other business that offers competing robotics or coding education programmes in Ghana without the prior written consent of the Company.

**10. TERMINATION**
10.1. Either party may terminate this Agreement by providing **[e.g., 14 (fourteen)]** days' written notice to the other.
10.2. The Company may terminate this Agreement immediately for cause if the Contractor commits a material breach of this Agreement, including but not limited to gross misconduct, repeated operational breaches, or theft of Company property.

**11. GOVERNING LAW**
This Agreement shall be governed by and construed in accordance with the laws of Ghana.

**IN WITNESS WHEREOF,** the parties have executed this Agreement as of the date first above written.

**SIGNED for and on behalf of**
**[YOUR COMPANY NAME LTD.]**
____________________________________
**Signature**
**Name: [Your Name/John's Name]**
**Title: Director**

**SIGNED by the CONTRACTOR**
____________________________________
**Signature**
**Name: [Facilitator's Name]**
**Date:**

---

### **ANNEXURE A: REFERENCE TO OPERATIONS MANUAL & THE "JOHN PROTOCOL"**

The Contractor is bound by the procedures and policies set forth in the Company's internal Operations Manual, which includes but is not limited to:

1.  **Equipment Management ("The John Protocol"):** The mandatory process for checking out, verifying on-site, and checking in all Company equipment before and after every Session, as supervised by the Oversight Manager.
2.  **Curriculum & Session Delivery:** The prescribed modules, teaching methods, and learning outcomes for all Sessions.
3.  **Use of Zobase System:** The mandatory process for clocking in and out to record work hours and Session completion.

*The latest version of the Operations Manual will be provided to the Contractor separately. The Company reserves the right to update the Manual, and the Contractor will be bound by such updates upon notification.*

---

### **Key Points for Your Solicitor:**

*   **Ghana Labour Act Compliance:** The preamble and Clause 2 are deliberately crafted to distinguish this from an employment relationship under Act 651. Please verify this provides sufficient protection.
*   **Annexure Strategy:** Using an "Annexure A" for the Operations Manual allows you to update operational details (like specific checklists) without needing to amend the core contract every time.
*   **Enforceability of Fines:** Clause 5 frames fines as "deductions," which is more legally sound than calling them "penalties." Please advise on the specific amounts and the overall enforceability of this clause under Ghanaian law.
*   **Non-Solicit & Exclusivity:** The duration and scope of Clauses 6 and 7 should be reviewed for reasonableness to ensure they are legally defensible.

    
---

### **DRAFT: SCHOOL PARTNERSHIP SERVICE AGREEMENT**

**THIS AGREEMENT** is made on **[Date]**

**BETWEEN:**

**[Your Company Name Ltd.]**, a company registered in Ghana with its registered office at **[Your Company Address]** (hereinafter referred to as "**the Service Provider**").

**AND**

**[School Name]**, a school located at **[School Address]** (hereinafter referred to as "**the School**").

Collectively referred to as "the Parties."

**BACKGROUND:**

A. The Service Provider is engaged in the business of providing robotics and coding education programmes.
B. The School wishes to engage the Service Provider to deliver its mBot Robotics courses to the School's students.
C. The Parties intend to define the terms under which these services will be provided.

**IT IS HEREBY AGREED AS FOLLOWS:**

**1. INTERPRETATION**
1.1. The "**Programme**" refers to the specific course (e.g., 10-Week Short Course or Year-Long Programme) as detailed in Annexure A.
1.2. "**Fees**" refer to the total cost for the Programme as specified in Clause 4.
1.3. "**IP**" means all intellectual property, including curriculum, software, teaching methods, and manuals.

**2. SERVICES AND TERM**
2.1. The Service Provider agrees to deliver the Programme to the School's students, in accordance with the schedule, location, and student capacity detailed in **Annexure A**.
2.2. The initial term of this Agreement shall be for the duration of the Programme as specified in Annexure A. It may be renewed by mutual written agreement of the Parties.

**3. LOGISTICS AND EQUIPMENT**
3.1. The Service Provider shall provide all necessary equipment, including but not limited to mBot robotics kits, laptops, sensors, and learning materials, required for the delivery of the Programme.
3.2. The School shall provide a suitable classroom or venue, with adequate seating, lighting, and security, for the duration of the scheduled sessions.

**4. FEES, REVENUE SHARE, AND PAYMENT**

4.1. **Student Enrollment and Fee Collection:**
    4.1.1. The Service Provider shall be solely responsible for the management of student enrollments and the collection of all enrollment fees ("**Gross Fees**") for the Programme.
    4.1.2. All Gross Fees shall be collected directly by the Service Provider via its designated payment channels, which include its official website and MTN Mobile Money merchant account (**[Insert Merchant Number]**).

4.2. **Revenue Share:**
    4.2.1. In consideration for the SchoolÃ¢â‚¬â„¢s provision of the venue, promotion of the Programme to its student body, and its partnership hereunder, the Service Provider shall pay the School a revenue share (the "**School Share**").
    4.2.2. The School Share shall be calculated as **[e.g., fifteen percent (15%)]** of the Net Revenue received by the Service Provider for each student enrolled from the School. For the purposes of this Agreement, "**Net Revenue**" is defined as the Gross Fees, excluding any direct transactional costs, taxes, or third-party payment processing fees.

4.3. **Payment of School Share:**
    4.3.1. The Service Provider shall provide the School with a detailed statement of enrollments and the calculated School Share within seven (7) days of the conclusion of the enrollment period for each Programme cycle.
    4.3.2. Payment of the School Share shall be made by the Service Provider to the School via bank transfer or mobile money to a designated account provided by the School in writing. Payment shall be made in full within fourteen (14) days of the commencement of the Programme.

4.4. **Audit Right:**
    The School shall have the right, once per calendar year and upon thirty (30) days' written notice, to appoint an independent auditor to inspect the Service ProviderÃ¢â‚¬â„¢s records solely relating to the calculation of the School Share for the preceding twelve (12) months. Such an audit shall be conducted at the SchoolÃ¢â‚¬â„¢s expense, unless it reveals an underpayment of more than five percent (5%), in which case the cost of the audit shall be borne by the Service Provider.

4.5. **School's Promotion Obligation:**
    The School agrees to actively promote the Programme to its parents and students through its standard communication channels, including but not limited to, newsletters, parent-teacher association meetings, and official school announcements. The Parties agree that the enrollment levels and resulting School Share are contingent upon this collaborative promotional effort.

**5. INTELLECTUAL PROPERTY**
5.1. All IP provided by the Service Provider in connection with this Agreement is and shall remain the sole and exclusive property of the Service Provider.
5.2. The School is granted a non-exclusive, non-transferable licence to use the IP solely for the purpose of receiving the Programme. The School shall not copy, modify, or distribute any of the Service Provider's IP.

**6. DATA PROTECTION**
Both Parties shall comply with the provisions of the **Ghana Data Protection Act, 2012 (Act 843)** in relation to any personal data of students, parents, or staff processed under this Agreement. Each Party shall ensure it has appropriate legal grounds for processing such data.

**7. LIMITATION OF LIABILITY**
7.1. The total aggregate liability of the Service Provider, whether in contract, tort (including negligence), or otherwise, arising out of or in connection with this Agreement shall be limited to the total Fees paid by the School under this Agreement.
7.2. The Service Provider shall not be liable to the School for any indirect, consequential, or special damages, including loss of profit or revenue.

**8. FORCE MAJEURE**
Neither Party shall be held liable for any failure or delay in the performance of its obligations under this Agreement if such failure or delay is due to any cause beyond its reasonable control, including but not limited to acts of God, war, terrorism, government regulations, or pandemic. In such an event, obligations shall be suspended for the duration of the force majeure, and if it persists for more than 30 days, either Party may terminate this Agreement.

**9. TERMINATION**
9.1. Either Party may terminate this Agreement with immediate effect by giving written notice to the other if the other Party commits a material breach of any term of this Agreement and (if such a breach is remediable) fails to remedy that breach within a period of **30 days** after being notified in writing to do so.
9.2. Upon termination, the School shall immediately pay any outstanding Fees for services already rendered.

**10. GOVERNING LAW AND DISPUTE RESOLUTION**
10.1. This Agreement shall be governed by and construed in accordance with the laws of Ghana.
10.2. Any dispute arising from this Agreement shall be settled first through mediation, and if unsuccessful, by the courts of Ghana.

**IN WITNESS WHEREOF,** the parties have executed this Agreement as of the date first above written.

**SIGNED for and on behalf of**
**[YOUR COMPANY NAME LTD.]**
____________________________________
**Signature**
**Name: [Your Name]**
**Title: Director**

**SIGNED for and on behalf of**
**[SCHOOL NAME]**
____________________________________
**Signature**
**Name: [School Representative's Name]**
**Title: [e.g., Headmaster/Proprietor]**

---

### **ANNEXURE A: PROGRAMME DETAILS**

**1. Programme Type:**
- [ ] 10-Week Short Course
- [ ] Year-Long Programme (After-School & Holiday Camp)

**2. Programme Schedule:**
- Start Date: [Date]
- End Date: [Date]
- Session Days & Times: [e.g., Tuesdays & Thursdays, 3:00 PM - 4:00 PM]

**3. Location:**
- Venue: [School Name/Designated Room]
- Address: [Full Address of Session Venue]

**4. Student Capacity:**
- Maximum Number of Students: [e.g., 20]

**5. Total Fees:**
- Amount: [e.g., GHS 20,000]
- Payment Due Date: [Date]

---
### **Key Points for Your Solicitor's Review:**

*   **Payment Security:** Clause 4 is structured to secure payment upfront via a traceable method, critical for your cash flow.
*   **IP Protection:** Clause 5 is broad and definitive to prevent the school from replicating your core business offering.
*   **Liability Cap:** Clause 7 is a standard but vital risk-management tool to prevent catastrophic liability.
*   **Annexure Flexibility:** Using Annexure A allows for the specifics of each school partnership (schedule, fees) to be detailed without altering the main body of the contract.
*   **Data Protection:** Clause 6 ensures compliance with Act 843, a necessary safeguard.



   

    Liability waivers.

    Fee structure and payment schedule to you.

    Data sharing agreements (for student rosters).
---

### **DRAFT: SCHOOL PARTNERSHIP SERVICE AGREEMENT**

**THIS AGREEMENT** is made on **[Date]**

**BETWEEN:**

**[Your Company Name Ltd.]**, a company registered in Ghana with its registered office at **[Your Company Address]** (hereinafter referred to as "**the Service Provider**").

**AND**

**[School Name]**, a school located at **[School Address]** (hereinafter referred to as "**the School**").

Collectively referred to as "the Parties."

**BACKGROUND:**

A. The Service Provider is engaged in the business of providing robotics and coding education programmes.
B. The School wishes to engage the Service Provider to deliver its mBot Robotics courses to the School's students.
C. The Parties intend to define the terms under which these services will be provided.

**IT IS HEREBY AGREED AS FOLLOWS:**

**1. INTERPRETATION**
1.1. The "**Programme**" refers to the specific course (e.g., 10-Week Short Course or Year-Long Programme) as detailed in Annexure A.
1.2. "**Fees**" refer to the total cost for the Programme as specified in Clause 4.
1.3. "**IP**" means all intellectual property, including curriculum, software, teaching methods, and manuals.

**2. SERVICES AND TERM**
2.1. The Service Provider agrees to deliver the Programme to the School's students, in accordance with the schedule, location, and student capacity detailed in **Annexure A**.
2.2. The initial term of this Agreement shall be for the duration of the Programme as specified in Annexure A. It may be renewed by mutual written agreement of the Parties.

**3. LOGISTICS AND EQUIPMENT**
3.1. The Service Provider shall provide all necessary equipment, including but not limited to mBot robotics kits, laptops, sensors, and learning materials, required for the delivery of the Programme.
3.2. The School shall provide a suitable classroom or venue, with adequate seating, lighting, and security, for the duration of the scheduled sessions.

**4. FEES, REVENUE SHARE, AND PAYMENT**

4.1. **Student Enrollment and Fee Collection:**
    4.1.1. The Service Provider shall be solely responsible for the management of student enrollments and the collection of all enrollment fees ("**Gross Fees**") for the Programme.
    4.1.2. All Gross Fees shall be collected directly by the Service Provider via its designated payment channels, which include its official website and MTN Mobile Money merchant account (**[Insert Merchant Number]**).

4.2. **Revenue Share:**
    4.2.1. In consideration for the Schoolâ€™s provision of the venue, promotion of the Programme to its student body, and its partnership hereunder, the Service Provider shall pay the School a revenue share (the "**School Share**").
    4.2.2. The School Share shall be calculated as **[e.g., fifteen percent (15%)]** of the Net Revenue received by the Service Provider for each student enrolled from the School. For the purposes of this Agreement, "**Net Revenue**" is defined as the Gross Fees, excluding any direct transactional costs, taxes, or third-party payment processing fees.

4.3. **Payment of School Share:**
    4.3.1. The Service Provider shall provide the School with a detailed statement of enrollments and the calculated School Share within seven (7) days of the conclusion of the enrollment period for each Programme cycle.
    4.3.2. Payment of the School Share shall be made by the Service Provider to the School via bank transfer or mobile money to a designated account provided by the School in writing. Payment shall be made in full within fourteen (14) days of the commencement of the Programme.

4.4. **Audit Right:**
    The School shall have the right, once per calendar year and upon thirty (30) days' written notice, to appoint an independent auditor to inspect the Service Providerâ€™s records solely relating to the calculation of the School Share for the preceding twelve (12) months. Such an audit shall be conducted at the Schoolâ€™s expense, unless it reveals an underpayment of more than five percent (5%), in which case the cost of the audit shall be borne by the Service Provider.

4.5. **School's Promotion Obligation:**
    The School agrees to actively promote the Programme to its parents and students through its standard communication channels, including but not limited to, newsletters, parent-teacher association meetings, and official school announcements. The Parties agree that the enrollment levels and resulting School Share are contingent upon this collaborative promotional effort.

**5. INTELLECTUAL PROPERTY**
5.1. All IP provided by the Service Provider in connection with this Agreement is and shall remain the sole and exclusive property of the Service Provider.
5.2. The School is granted a non-exclusive, non-transferable licence to use the IP solely for the purpose of receiving the Programme. The School shall not copy, modify, or distribute any of the Service Provider's IP.

**6. DATA PROTECTION**
Both Parties shall comply with the provisions of the **Ghana Data Protection Act, 2012 (Act 843)** in relation to any personal data of students, parents, or staff processed under this Agreement. Each Party shall ensure it has appropriate legal grounds for processing such data.

**7. LIMITATION OF LIABILITY**
7.1. The total aggregate liability of the Service Provider, whether in contract, tort (including negligence), or otherwise, arising out of or in connection with this Agreement shall be limited to the total Fees paid by the School under this Agreement.
7.2. The Service Provider shall not be liable to the School for any indirect, consequential, or special damages, including loss of profit or revenue.

**8. FORCE MAJEURE**
Neither Party shall be held liable for any failure or delay in the performance of its obligations under this Agreement if such failure or delay is due to any cause beyond its reasonable control, including but not limited to acts of God, war, terrorism, government regulations, or pandemic. In such an event, obligations shall be suspended for the duration of the force majeure, and if it persists for more than 30 days, either Party may terminate this Agreement.

**9. TERMINATION**
9.1. Either Party may terminate this Agreement with immediate effect by giving written notice to the other if the other Party commits a material breach of any term of this Agreement and (if such a breach is remediable) fails to remedy that breach within a period of **30 days** after being notified in writing to do so.
9.2. Upon termination, the School shall immediately pay any outstanding Fees for services already rendered.

**10. GOVERNING LAW AND DISPUTE RESOLUTION**
10.1. This Agreement shall be governed by and construed in accordance with the laws of Ghana.
10.2. Any dispute arising from this Agreement shall be settled first through mediation, and if unsuccessful, by the courts of Ghana.

**IN WITNESS WHEREOF,** the parties have executed this Agreement as of the date first above written.

**SIGNED for and on behalf of**
**[YOUR COMPANY NAME LTD.]**
____________________________________
**Signature**
**Name: [Your Name]**
**Title: Director**

**SIGNED for and on behalf of**
**[SCHOOL NAME]**
____________________________________
**Signature**
**Name: [School Representative's Name]**
**Title: [e.g., Headmaster/Proprietor]**

---

### **ANNEXURE A: PROGRAMME DETAILS**

**1. Programme Type:**
- [ ] 10-Week Short Course
- [ ] Year-Long Programme (After-School & Holiday Camp)

**2. Programme Schedule:**
- Start Date: [Date]
- End Date: [Date]
- Session Days & Times: [e.g., Tuesdays & Thursdays, 3:00 PM - 4:00 PM]

**3. Location:**
- Venue: [School Name/Designated Room]
- Address: [Full Address of Session Venue]

**4. Student Capacity:**
- Maximum Number of Students: [e.g., 20]

**5. Total Fees:**
- Amount: [e.g., GHS 20,000]
- Payment Due Date: [Date]

---
### **Key Points for Your Solicitor's Review:**

*   **Payment Security:** Clause 4 is structured to secure payment upfront via a traceable method, critical for your cash flow.
*   **IP Protection:** Clause 5 is broad and definitive to prevent the school from replicating your core business offering.
*   **Liability Cap:** Clause 7 is a standard but vital risk-management tool to prevent catastrophic liability.
*   **Annexure Flexibility:** Using Annexure A allows for the specifics of each school partnership (schedule, fees) to be detailed without altering the main body of the contract.
*   **Data Protection:** Clause 6 ensures compliance with Act 843, a necessary safeguard.



   

    Liability waivers.

    Fee structure and payment schedule to you.

    Data sharing agreements (for student rosters).
---

### **DRAFT: FACILITATOR INDEPENDENT CONTRACTOR AGREEMENT**

**THIS AGREEMENT** is made on **[Date]**

**BETWEEN:**

**[Your Company Name Ltd.]**, a company registered in Ghana with its registered office at **[Your Company Address]** (hereinafter referred to as "**the Company**").

**AND**

**[Facilitator's Full Name]**, of **[Facilitator's Address]** (hereinafter referred to as "**the Contractor**").

**BACKGROUND:**

The Company is engaged in the business of providing robotics and coding education. The Company wishes to engage the services of the Contractor as a facilitator for its educational programmes, and the Contractor agrees to provide such services, on the terms and conditions set forth in this Agreement.

**IT IS HEREBY AGREED AS FOLLOWS:**

**1. INTERPRETATION**
The terms "Commission," "Equipment," "Operations Manual," "Oversight Manager," "Session," and "Zobase System" shall have the meanings ascribed to them in this Agreement and its Annexures.

**2. STATUS AS INDEPENDENT CONTRACTOR**
2.1. The Contractor is engaged as an independent contractor. This Agreement shall not be construed as creating a relationship of employer-employee, partnership, or joint venture between the Parties.
2.2. The Contractor acknowledges that they are solely responsible for their own income tax, National Health Insurance, and other statutory obligations, and are not entitled to any benefits provided by the Company to its employees, including but not limited to contributions under the Social Security and National Insurance Trust (SSNIT).
2.3. The Contractor has no authority to bind the Company in any way without prior written consent.

**3. ROLES, RESPONSIBILITIES & COMMISSION**
3.1. **Duties:** The Contractor shall:
    3.1.1. Facilitate robotics and coding Sessions for students in accordance with the curriculum, schedules, and procedures detailed in the Company's Operations Manual (Annexure A).
    3.1.2. Adhere strictly to the equipment management, check-in, and check-out procedures ("The John Protocol") as directed by the Oversight Manager.
    3.1.3. Represent the Company professionally at all times.
3.2. **Commission Structure:**
    3.2.1. The Contractor's remuneration shall be solely on a commission-only basis.
    3.2.2. The commission rate shall be **[e.g., 40%]** of the net fee received by the Company for each Session that is successfully completed by the Contractor.
    3.2.3. A Session is deemed "successfully completed" only upon the Oversight Manager's confirmation that: (i) the Session was delivered; (ii) all equipment was returned undamaged; and (iii) the post-session checklist has been signed.
3.3. **Oversight:** The Contractor shall report to and comply with all reasonable instructions from the Company's designated Oversight Manager ("John") regarding operational procedures.

**4. EQUIPMENT ACCOUNTABILITY & INTELLECTUAL PROPERTY**
4.1. The Contractor is financially accountable for any loss or damage to Company Equipment assigned to them. The Contractor agrees to reimburse the Company the full replacement value for any lost or damaged equipment, which may be deducted from accrued commissions.
4.2. All curriculum, training materials, processes, and any other intellectual property created by the Contractor in the course of, or related to, this Agreement shall be the sole and exclusive property of the Company. The Contractor hereby assigns all rights, title, and interest in such intellectual property to the Company.


5. Offsite Mismanagement & Misuse

5.1. The Contractor shall not use the Company's Equipment for any purpose other than the performance of the Services at the locations expressly prescribed by the Company in writing ("Prescribed Sites"). Any use of Equipment for personal projects, non-approved sessions, or at any location other than a Prescribed Site is strictly prohibited and shall constitute material misuse.

5.2. The Contractor assumes full financial responsibility for any loss, damage, or repair costs arising from misuse, improper handling, or use of the Equipment at a non-Prescribed Site. The Company is authorized to deduct the full replacement or repair value from the Contractor's pending commissions.

5.3. Any breach of this clause shall be deemed a material breach of this Agreement, entitling the Company to terminate the Agreement immediately and pursue any and all available legal remedies



6. Training Obligation (Main Facilitator)

6.1. The Contractor, in their capacity as Main Facilitator, agrees to provide practical training to all new facilitators engaged by the Company. This training shall ensure new personnel are fully equipped to perform their duties competently and in compliance with the Company's curriculum and operational protocols.

6.2. The training shall be tailored to the specific roles and responsibilities of a facilitator and shall include, but not be limited to, instruction on:
(a) The Company's prescribed curriculum and teaching methodologies;
(b) Equipment management, including the "John Protocol" for check-in and check-out procedures;
(c) All operational and safety procedures as outlined in the Company's Operations Manual.

6.3. The Main Facilitator shall perform these training duties at the Company's reasonable request and as an integral part of the Services rendered under this Agreement, for which the Main Facilitator's standard commission shall be the sole compensation.




**7. SCHEDULE OF FINES**
The Company may impose the following fines for operational breaches, which shall be deducted from the Contractor's commission:

| Breach | Fine Amount |
| :--- | :--- |
| Failure to clock in/out on the Zobase System for a Session without prior authorization. | [e.g., Ã¢â€šÂµ50] |
| Failure to complete the pre- or post-session equipment checklist. | [e.g., Ã¢â€šÂµ75] |
| Unauthorized use of Company Equipment or deviation from the prescribed curriculum. | [e.g., Ã¢â€šÂµ100] |
| Unauthorized use of a Session venue. | [e.g., Ã¢â€šÂµ200] |

**8. CONFIDENTIALITY & NON-SOLICITATION**
8.1. The Contractor shall not, during or after the termination of this Agreement, disclose any confidential information of the Company, including but not limited to business strategies, client lists, pricing, and curriculum.
8.2. **Non-Solicitation:** The Contractor shall not, for a period of **[e.g., 12 (twelve)]** months following the termination of this Agreement, directly or indirectly solicit or entice away any client, student, or employee of the Company.

**9. EXCLUSIVITY**
During the term of this Agreement, the Contractor shall not provide services, whether as an employee or contractor, to any other business that offers competing robotics or coding education programmes in Ghana without the prior written consent of the Company.

**10. TERMINATION**
10.1. Either party may terminate this Agreement by providing **[e.g., 14 (fourteen)]** days' written notice to the other.
10.2. The Company may terminate this Agreement immediately for cause if the Contractor commits a material breach of this Agreement, including but not limited to gross misconduct, repeated operational breaches, or theft of Company property.

**11. GOVERNING LAW**
This Agreement shall be governed by and construed in accordance with the laws of Ghana.

**IN WITNESS WHEREOF,** the parties have executed this Agreement as of the date first above written.

**SIGNED for and on behalf of**
**[YOUR COMPANY NAME LTD.]**
____________________________________
**Signature**
**Name: [Your Name/John's Name]**
**Title: Director**

**SIGNED by the CONTRACTOR**
____________________________________
**Signature**
**Name: [Facilitator's Name]**
**Date:**

---

### **ANNEXURE A: REFERENCE TO OPERATIONS MANUAL & THE "JOHN PROTOCOL"**

The Contractor is bound by the procedures and policies set forth in the Company's internal Operations Manual, which includes but is not limited to:

1.  **Equipment Management ("The John Protocol"):** The mandatory process for checking out, verifying on-site, and checking in all Company equipment before and after every Session, as supervised by the Oversight Manager.
2.  **Curriculum & Session Delivery:** The prescribed modules, teaching methods, and learning outcomes for all Sessions.
3.  **Use of Zobase System:** The mandatory process for clocking in and out to record work hours and Session completion.

*The latest version of the Operations Manual will be provided to the Contractor separately. The Company reserves the right to update the Manual, and the Contractor will be bound by such updates upon notification.*

---

### **Key Points for Your Solicitor:**

*   **Ghana Labour Act Compliance:** The preamble and Clause 2 are deliberately crafted to distinguish this from an employment relationship under Act 651. Please verify this provides sufficient protection.
*   **Annexure Strategy:** Using an "Annexure A" for the Operations Manual allows you to update operational details (like specific checklists) without needing to amend the core contract every time.
*   **Enforceability of Fines:** Clause 5 frames fines as "deductions," which is more legally sound than calling them "penalties." Please advise on the specific amounts and the overall enforceability of this clause under Ghanaian law.
*   **Non-Solicit & Exclusivity:** The duration and scope of Clauses 6 and 7 should be reviewed for reasonableness to ensure they are legally defensible.

    
---

### **DRAFT: SCHOOL PARTNERSHIP SERVICE AGREEMENT**

**THIS AGREEMENT** is made on **[Date]**

**BETWEEN:**

**[Your Company Name Ltd.]**, a company registered in Ghana with its registered office at **[Your Company Address]** (hereinafter referred to as "**the Service Provider**").

**AND**

**[School Name]**, a school located at **[School Address]** (hereinafter referred to as "**the School**").

Collectively referred to as "the Parties."

**BACKGROUND:**

A. The Service Provider is engaged in the business of providing robotics and coding education programmes.
B. The School wishes to engage the Service Provider to deliver its mBot Robotics courses to the School's students.
C. The Parties intend to define the terms under which these services will be provided.

**IT IS HEREBY AGREED AS FOLLOWS:**

**1. INTERPRETATION**
1.1. The "**Programme**" refers to the specific course (e.g., 10-Week Short Course or Year-Long Programme) as detailed in Annexure A.
1.2. "**Fees**" refer to the total cost for the Programme as specified in Clause 4.
1.3. "**IP**" means all intellectual property, including curriculum, software, teaching methods, and manuals.

**2. SERVICES AND TERM**
2.1. The Service Provider agrees to deliver the Programme to the School's students, in accordance with the schedule, location, and student capacity detailed in **Annexure A**.
2.2. The initial term of this Agreement shall be for the duration of the Programme as specified in Annexure A. It may be renewed by mutual written agreement of the Parties.

**3. LOGISTICS AND EQUIPMENT**
3.1. The Service Provider shall provide all necessary equipment, including but not limited to mBot robotics kits, laptops, sensors, and learning materials, required for the delivery of the Programme.
3.2. The School shall provide a suitable classroom or venue, with adequate seating, lighting, and security, for the duration of the scheduled sessions.

**4. FEES, REVENUE SHARE, AND PAYMENT**

4.1. **Student Enrollment and Fee Collection:**
    4.1.1. The Service Provider shall be solely responsible for the management of student enrollments and the collection of all enrollment fees ("**Gross Fees**") for the Programme.
    4.1.2. All Gross Fees shall be collected directly by the Service Provider via its designated payment channels, which include its official website and MTN Mobile Money merchant account (**[Insert Merchant Number]**).

4.2. **Revenue Share:**
    4.2.1. In consideration for the SchoolÃ¢â‚¬â„¢s provision of the venue, promotion of the Programme to its student body, and its partnership hereunder, the Service Provider shall pay the School a revenue share (the "**School Share**").
    4.2.2. The School Share shall be calculated as **[e.g., fifteen percent (15%)]** of the Net Revenue received by the Service Provider for each student enrolled from the School. For the purposes of this Agreement, "**Net Revenue**" is defined as the Gross Fees, excluding any direct transactional costs, taxes, or third-party payment processing fees.

4.3. **Payment of School Share:**
    4.3.1. The Service Provider shall provide the School with a detailed statement of enrollments and the calculated School Share within seven (7) days of the conclusion of the enrollment period for each Programme cycle.
    4.3.2. Payment of the School Share shall be made by the Service Provider to the School via bank transfer or mobile money to a designated account provided by the School in writing. Payment shall be made in full within fourteen (14) days of the commencement of the Programme.

4.4. **Audit Right:**
    The School shall have the right, once per calendar year and upon thirty (30) days' written notice, to appoint an independent auditor to inspect the Service ProviderÃ¢â‚¬â„¢s records solely relating to the calculation of the School Share for the preceding twelve (12) months. Such an audit shall be conducted at the SchoolÃ¢â‚¬â„¢s expense, unless it reveals an underpayment of more than five percent (5%), in which case the cost of the audit shall be borne by the Service Provider.

4.5. **School's Promotion Obligation:**
    The School agrees to actively promote the Programme to its parents and students through its standard communication channels, including but not limited to, newsletters, parent-teacher association meetings, and official school announcements. The Parties agree that the enrollment levels and resulting School Share are contingent upon this collaborative promotional effort.

**5. INTELLECTUAL PROPERTY**
5.1. All IP provided by the Service Provider in connection with this Agreement is and shall remain the sole and exclusive property of the Service Provider.
5.2. The School is granted a non-exclusive, non-transferable licence to use the IP solely for the purpose of receiving the Programme. The School shall not copy, modify, or distribute any of the Service Provider's IP.

**6. DATA PROTECTION**
Both Parties shall comply with the provisions of the **Ghana Data Protection Act, 2012 (Act 843)** in relation to any personal data of students, parents, or staff processed under this Agreement. Each Party shall ensure it has appropriate legal grounds for processing such data.

**7. LIMITATION OF LIABILITY**
7.1. The total aggregate liability of the Service Provider, whether in contract, tort (including negligence), or otherwise, arising out of or in connection with this Agreement shall be limited to the total Fees paid by the School under this Agreement.
7.2. The Service Provider shall not be liable to the School for any indirect, consequential, or special damages, including loss of profit or revenue.

**8. FORCE MAJEURE**
Neither Party shall be held liable for any failure or delay in the performance of its obligations under this Agreement if such failure or delay is due to any cause beyond its reasonable control, including but not limited to acts of God, war, terrorism, government regulations, or pandemic. In such an event, obligations shall be suspended for the duration of the force majeure, and if it persists for more than 30 days, either Party may terminate this Agreement.

**9. TERMINATION**
9.1. Either Party may terminate this Agreement with immediate effect by giving written notice to the other if the other Party commits a material breach of any term of this Agreement and (if such a breach is remediable) fails to remedy that breach within a period of **30 days** after being notified in writing to do so.
9.2. Upon termination, the School shall immediately pay any outstanding Fees for services already rendered.

**10. GOVERNING LAW AND DISPUTE RESOLUTION**
10.1. This Agreement shall be governed by and construed in accordance with the laws of Ghana.
10.2. Any dispute arising from this Agreement shall be settled first through mediation, and if unsuccessful, by the courts of Ghana.

**IN WITNESS WHEREOF,** the parties have executed this Agreement as of the date first above written.

**SIGNED for and on behalf of**
**[YOUR COMPANY NAME LTD.]**
____________________________________
**Signature**
**Name: [Your Name]**
**Title: Director**

**SIGNED for and on behalf of**
**[SCHOOL NAME]**
____________________________________
**Signature**
**Name: [School Representative's Name]**
**Title: [e.g., Headmaster/Proprietor]**

---

### **ANNEXURE A: PROGRAMME DETAILS**

**1. Programme Type:**
- [ ] 10-Week Short Course
- [ ] Year-Long Programme (After-School & Holiday Camp)

**2. Programme Schedule:**
- Start Date: [Date]
- End Date: [Date]
- Session Days & Times: [e.g., Tuesdays & Thursdays, 3:00 PM - 4:00 PM]

**3. Location:**
- Venue: [School Name/Designated Room]
- Address: [Full Address of Session Venue]

**4. Student Capacity:**
- Maximum Number of Students: [e.g., 20]

**5. Total Fees:**
- Amount: [e.g., GHS 20,000]
- Payment Due Date: [Date]

---
### **Key Points for Your Solicitor's Review:**

*   **Payment Security:** Clause 4 is structured to secure payment upfront via a traceable method, critical for your cash flow.
*   **IP Protection:** Clause 5 is broad and definitive to prevent the school from replicating your core business offering.
*   **Liability Cap:** Clause 7 is a standard but vital risk-management tool to prevent catastrophic liability.
*   **Annexure Flexibility:** Using Annexure A allows for the specifics of each school partnership (schedule, fees) to be detailed without altering the main body of the contract.
*   **Data Protection:** Clause 6 ensures compliance with Act 843, a necessary safeguard.



   

    Liability waivers.

    Fee structure and payment schedule to you.

    Data sharing agreements (for student rosters).

6.1. The Contractor, in their capacity as Main Facilitator, agrees to provide practical training to all new facilitators engaged by the Company. This training shall ensure new personnel are fully equipped to perform their duties competently and in compliance with the Company's curriculum and operational protocols.

6.2. The training shall be tailored to the specific roles and responsibilities of a facilitator and shall include, but not be limited to, instruction on:
(a) The Company's prescribed curriculum and teaching methodologies;
(b) Equipment management, including the "John Protocol" for check-in and check-out procedures;
(c) All operational and safety procedures as outlined in the Company's Operations Manual.

6.3. The Main Facilitator shall perform these training duties at the Company's reasonable request and as an integral part of the Services rendered under this Agreement, for which the Main Facilitator's standard commission shall be the sole compensation.




**7. SCHEDULE OF FINES**
The Company may impose the following fines for operational breaches, which shall be deducted from the Contractor's commission:

| Breach | Fine Amount |
| :--- | :--- |
| Failure to clock in/out on the Zobase System for a Session without prior authorization. | [e.g., â‚µ50] |

**8. CONFIDENTIALITY & NON-SOLICITATION**
8.1. The Contractor shall not, during or after the termination of this Agreement, disclose any confidential information of the Company, including but not limited to business strategies, client lists, pricing, and curriculum.
8.2. **Non-Solicitation:** The Contractor shall not, for a period of **[e.g., 12 (twelve)]** months following the termination of this Agreement, directly or indirectly solicit or entice away any client, student, or employee of the Company.

**9. EXCLUSIVITY**
During the term of this Agreement, the Contractor shall not provide services, whether as an employee or contractor, to any other business that offers competing robotics or coding education programmes in Ghana without the prior written consent of the Company.

10.1. Either party may terminate this Agreement by providing **[e.g., 14 (fourteen)]** days' written notice to the other.

This Agreement shall be governed by and construed in accordance with the laws of Ghana.

The Contractor is bound by the procedures and policies set forth in the Company's internal Operations Manual, which includes but is not limited to:

*   **Non-Solicit & Exclusivity:** The duration and scope of Clauses 6 and 7 should be reviewed for reasonableness to ensure they are legally defensible.

    4.2.2. The School Share shall be calculated as **[e.g., fifteen percent (15%)]** of the Net Revenue received by the Service Provider for each student enrolled from the School. For the purposes of this Agreement, "**Net Revenue**" is defined as the Gross Fees, excluding any direct transactional costs, taxes, or third-party payment processing fees.

4.3. **Payment of School Share:**
    4.3.1. The Service Provider shall provide the School with a detailed statement of enrollments and the calculated School Share within seven (7) days of the conclusion of the enrollment period for each Programme cycle.
    4.3.2. Payment of the School Share shall be made by the Service Provider to the School via bank transfer or mobile money to a designated account provided by the School in writing. Payment shall be made in full within fourteen (14) days of the commencement of the Programme.

4.4. **Audit Right:**
    The School shall have the right, once per calendar year and upon thirty (30) days' written notice, to appoint an independent auditor to inspect the Service Providerâ€™s records solely relating to the calculation of the School Share for the preceding twelve (12) months. Such an audit shall be conducted at the Schoolâ€™s expense, unless it reveals an underpayment of more than five percent (5%), in which case the cost of the audit shall be borne by the Service Provider.

4.5. **School's Promotion Obligation:**
    The School agrees to actively promote the Programme to its parents and students through its standard communication channels, including but not limited to, newsletters, parent-teacher association meetings, and official school announcements. The Parties agree that the enrollment levels and resulting School Share are contingent upon this collaborative promotional effort.

**5. INTELLECTUAL PROPERTY**
5.1. All IP provided by the Service Provider in connection with this Agreement is and shall remain the sole and exclusive property of the Service Provider.
5.2. The School is granted a non-exclusive, non-transferable licence to use the IP solely for the purpose of receiving the Programme. The School shall not copy, modify, or distribute any of the Service Provider's IP.

**6. DATA PROTECTION**
Both Parties shall comply with the provisions of the **Ghana Data Protection Act, 2012 (Act 843)** in relation to any personal data of students, parents, or staff processed under this Agreement. Each Party shall ensure it has appropriate legal grounds for processing such data.

**7. LIMITATION OF LIABILITY**
7.1. The total aggregate liability of the Service Provider, whether in contract, tort (including negligence), or otherwise, arising out of or in connection with this Agreement shall be limited to the total Fees paid by the School under this Agreement.
7.2. The Service Provider shall not be liable to the School for any indirect, consequential, or special damages, including loss of profit or revenue.

**8. FORCE MAJEURE**
Neither Party shall be held liable for any failure or delay in the performance of its obligations under this Agreement if such failure or delay is due to any cause beyond its reasonable control, including but not limited to acts of God, war, terrorism, government regulations, or pandemic. In such an event, obligations shall be suspended for the duration of the force majeure, and if it persists for more than 30 days, either Party may terminate this Agreement.

**9. TERMINATION**
9.1. Either Party may terminate this Agreement with immediate effect by giving written notice to the other if the other Party commits a material breach of any term of this Agreement and (if such a breach is remediable) fails to remedy that breach within a period of **30 days** after being notified in writing to do so.
9.2. Upon termination, the School shall immediately pay any outstanding Fees for services already rendered.

**10. GOVERNING LAW AND DISPUTE RESOLUTION**
10.1. This Agreement shall be governed by and construed in accordance with the laws of Ghana.
10.2. Any dispute arising from this Agreement shall be settled first through mediation, and if unsuccessful, by the courts of Ghana.

**IN WITNESS WHEREOF,** the parties have executed this Agreement as of the date first above written.

### **Key Points for Your Solicitor's Review:**

*   **Payment Security:** Clause 4 is structured to secure payment upfront via a traceable method, critical for your cash flow.
*   **IP Protection:** Clause 5 is broad and definitive to prevent the school from replicating your core business offering.
*   **Liability Cap:** Clause 7 is a standard but vital risk-management tool to prevent catastrophic liability.
*   **Annexure Flexibility:** Using Annexure A allows for the specifics of each school partnership (schedule, fees) to be detailed without altering the main body of the contract.
*   **Data Protection:** Clause 6 ensures compliance with Act 843, a necessary safeguard.
